'use strict';
const expect = require('/app/node_modules/chai').expect;
const sinon = require('/app/node_modules/sinon');
const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;
const Config = require('/app/lib/config');
const Reporter = require('/app/lib/utils/reporter');
const App = require('/app/lib/app');
const Server = require('/app/lib/server');
const BrowserTestRunner = require('/app/lib/runners/browser_test_runner');
const ProcessTestRunner = require('/app/lib/runners/process_test_runner');
const TapProcessTestRunner = require('/app/lib/runners/tap_process_test_runner');
const TapReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/tap_reporter');
const DotReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/dot_reporter');
const TeamcityReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter');
const XUnitReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/xunit_reporter');
const Launcher = require('/app/lib/launcher');
const FakeReporter = require('/app/tests/support/fake_reporter');
const FakeSocket = require('/app/tests/support/fake_socket');

function mockApp(bail) {
  return {
    config: {
      get: function(key) {
        switch (key) {
          case 'reporter': return new FakeReporter();
          case 'bail_on_test_failure': return bail;
          case 'xunit_intermediate_output': return false;
          default: return undefined;
        }
      }
    }
  };
}

describe('bail feature', function() {
  let sandbox, stream;
  beforeEach(function() { sandbox = sinon.createSandbox(); stream = new PassThrough(); });
  afterEach(function() { sandbox.restore(); });

  describe('Reporter', function() {
    it('extends EventEmitter', function() {
      expect(Reporter.prototype instanceof require('events').EventEmitter).to.be.true;
    });

    it('bails on first failure when true', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.true;
      expect(r.bailReason).to.equal('t1');
      expect(r.bailLauncher).to.equal('launcher');
      expect(r.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(0);
      let report = r.getBailReport();
      expect(report.bailLauncher).to.equal('launcher');
      expect(report.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({launcher: 1});
      expect(report.failedTests).to.deep.equal(['t1']);
    });

    it('bails on Nth failure per launcher', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(2), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
      r.report('other', {name: 't2', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't3', passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.true;
      expect(r.bailReason).to.equal('t3');
      expect(r.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(2);
    });

    it('does not count skipped/todo as failures', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 's', skipped: true, passed: false});
      r.report('launcher', {name: 't', todo: true, passed: false});
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
    });

    it('gates and counts suppressed after bail', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let sub = sandbox.stub(r.reporters[0], 'report');
      r.report('launcher', {name: 'fail', passed: false});
      r.report('launcher', {name: 'after', passed: true});
      expect(r.suppressed).to.equal(1);
      expect(sub.callCount).to.equal(1);
    });

    it('validates config and warns', function() {
      let warn = sandbox.stub(require('/app/node_modules/npmlog'), 'warn');
      let r0 = new Reporter(mockApp(0), stream);
      expect(r0.bailOnFailures).to.equal(false);
      expect(warn.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure')).to.be.true;
      warn.resetHistory();
      new Reporter(mockApp('yes'), stream);
      expect(warn.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure')).to.be.true;
      warn.resetHistory();
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(false), stream);
      expect(r.bailOnFailures).to.equal(false);
      expect(warn.called).to.be.false;
    });

    it('resetBailState clears', function() {
      let r = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      r.report('launcher', {name: 'fail', passed: false});
      r.resetBailState();
      expect(r.hasBailed()).to.be.false;
      expect(r.bailReason).to.equal(null);
      expect(r.bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
      expect(r.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(0);
      expect(r.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({});
      expect(r.failedTests).to.deep.equal([]);
      expect(r.suppressed).to.equal(0);
      let report = r.getBailReport();
      expect(report.bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
      expect(report.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({});
      expect(report.failedTests).to.deep.equal([]);
    });
  });

  describe('TAP reporter', function() {
    it('writes bail out and summary', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let tap = new TapReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      // simulate reporter reporting through aggregator
      reporterObj.reporters = [tap];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't2', passed: true});
      reporterObj.finish();
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain('Bail out! t1 (1)');
      expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
      expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 0');
      expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 1');
    });
  });

  describe('Dot reporter', function() {
    it('writes bail out and summary', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let dot = new DotReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      reporterObj.reporters = [dot];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      reporterObj.finish();
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain('Bail out! t1 (1)');
      expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
      expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 0');
      expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 0');
    });
  });

  describe('Teamcity reporter', function() {
    it('emits bail lines', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let tc = new TeamcityReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      reporterObj.reporters = [tc];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[message text='Bail out!' errorDetails='t1' status='ERROR']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildStatisticValue key='bailedTests' value='1']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildStatisticValue key='testsBeforeBail' value='0']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildStatisticValue key='suppressedAfterBail' value='0']");
      expect(out).to.contain("##teamcity[buildProblem");
    });
  });

  describe('XUnit reporter', function() {
    it('adds bail elements', function() {
      let reporterObj = new Reporter(mockApp(true), stream);
      let app = { reporter: reporterObj };
      let xu = new XUnitReporter(false, stream, new Config('ci'), app);
      reporterObj.reporters = [xu];
      reporterObj.report('launcher', {name: 't1', passed: false});
      reporterObj.finish();
      let out = stream.read().toString();
      expect(out).to.contain('errors="1"');
      expect(out).to.contain('<error');
      expect(out).to.contain('<properties>');
      expect(out).to.contain("name='bailReason' value='t1'");
      expect(out).to.contain("name='testsBeforeBail' value='0'");
      expect(out).to.contain("name='suppressedAfterBail' value='0'");
      expect(out).to.contain('<system-out>');
    });
  });

  describe('App', function() {
    it('getExitCode returns bail error', function() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      let app = new App(config);
      app.reporter = {
        hasBailed: function() { return true; },
        getBailReport: function() {
          return { bailReason: 'boom', testsRanBeforeBail: 5 };
        }
      };
      let e = app.getExitCode();
      expect(e.message).to.contain('Bail out!');
      expect(e.message).to.contain('boom');
      expect(e.message).to.contain('5');
      expect(e.message).not.to.contain('Not all tests passed');
    });

    it('abortRunners is idempotent and resets', function() {
      let config = new Config('ci');
      let app = new App(config);
      app.reporter = { resetBailState: sandbox.stub() };
      app.server = { broadcastAbort: sandbox.stub(), resetAbort: sandbox.stub() };
      let runner = { abort: sandbox.stub().returns(Promise.resolve()) };
      app.runners = [runner];
      let p1 = app.abortRunners();
      let p2 = app.abortRunners();
      expect(p1.then).to.be.a('function');
      expect(p2.then).to.be.a('function');
      expect(app.server.broadcastAbort.callCount).to.equal(1);
      expect(runner.abort.callCount).to.equal(1);
      app.resetBailState();
      expect(app.server.resetAbort.called).to.be.true;
      expect(app.aborted).to.equal(false);
    });
  });

  describe('Server', function() {
    it('broadcastAbort is idempotent and tolerates no io', function() {
      let s = new Server(new Config('ci'));
      expect(function() { s.broadcastAbort(); }).not.to.throw();
      s.io = { emit: sandbox.stub() };
      s.resetAbort();
      s.broadcastAbort();
      s.broadcastAbort();
      expect(s.io.emit.calledWith('abort-tests')).to.be.true;
      expect(s.io.emit.callCount).to.equal(1);
    });
  });

  describe('BrowserTestRunner', function() {
    it('abort is idempotent, emits abort-tests, suppresses results', function() {
      let reporter = new FakeReporter();
      let config = new Config('ci', { reporter: reporter });
      let launcher = new Launcher('ci', { protocol: 'browser' }, config);
      let runner = new BrowserTestRunner(launcher, reporter, null, null, config);
      let socket = new FakeSocket();
      runner.tryAttach('browser', launcher.id, socket);
      let p = runner.abort();
      expect(p.then).to.be.a('function');
      runner.abort();
      expect(socket.listeners('abort-tests')).to.have.length(1); // listener side
      runner.onTestResult({ failed: 1, name: 'x' });
      expect(reporter.results.length).to.equal(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Process runners', function() {
    it('abort is idempotent and suppresses', function() {
      let reporter = new FakeReporter();
      let config = new Config('ci', { reporter: reporter });
      let launcher = new Launcher('ci', { protocol: 'process' }, config);
      let runner = new ProcessTestRunner(launcher, reporter);
      let p = runner.abort();
      expect(p.then).to.be.a('function');
      runner.abort();
      runner.onProcessExit(1);
      expect(reporter.results.length).to.equal(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Client', function() {
    it('handleAbortTests sets aborted and blocks emitMessage', function() {
      let Testem = require('/app/public/testem/testem_client');
      Testem.aborted = false;
      Testem._isIframeReady = true;
      let calls = [];
      Testem.iframe = { contentWindow: { postMessage: function() { calls.push(arguments); } } };
      let local = [];
      Testem.on('abort-tests', function() { local.push('abort-tests'); });
      Testem.on('after-tests-complete', function() { local.push('after-tests-complete'); });
      Testem.handleAbortTests();
      expect(Testem.aborted).to.equal(true);
      Testem.emitMessage('test-result', {name: 'x'});
      expect(calls.length).to.equal(2);
      expect(local).to.include('abort-tests');
      expect(local).to.include('after-tests-complete');
    });
  });
});

  return { config: { get: function(key) {

COMMAND:
cat > /tmp/bail_adapter_test.js <<'EOF'
'use strict';
const expect = require('/app/node_modules/chai').expect;
const sinon = require('/app/node_modules/sinon');
const mochaAdapter = require('/app/public/testem/mocha_adapter');
const jasmine2Adapter = require('/app/public/testem/jasmine2_adapter');
const qunitAdapter = require('/app/public/testem/qunit_adapter');

function replaceGlobals(newGlobals, originalGlobals) {
  for (let key in newGlobals) {
    originalGlobals[key] = global[key];
    global[key] = newGlobals[key];
  }
}
function restoreGlobals(originalGlobals) {
  for (let key in originalGlobals) {
    global[key] = originalGlobals[key];
  }
}

function Runner() {}
Runner.prototype.emit = function() {};

describe('adapter abort guards', function() {
  let sandbox, globals, emit;

  beforeEach(function() {
    globals = {};
    sandbox = sinon.createSandbox();
    emit = sandbox.stub();
    global.Testem = { aborted: true };
    replaceGlobals({ emit: emit }, globals);
  });
  afterEach(function() {
    delete global.Testem;
    restoreGlobals(globals);
    sandbox.restore();
  });

  it('mocha suppresses and signals all-test-results once', function() {
    replaceGlobals({ mocha: { Runner: Runner }, Mocha: { Runner: Runner }, setTimeout: function(fn){ fn(); } }, globals);
    mochaAdapter();
    let runner = new Runner();
    runner.emit('start', {title:'a'});
    runner.emit('test end', {title:'b', state:'passed'});
    runner.emit('end', {});
    runner.emit('fail', {title:'c'}, {message:'x'});
    runner.emit('test end', {title:'d', state:'failed'});
    // all-test-results should be emitted exactly once
    let all = emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'all-test-results');
    expect(all.length).to.equal(1);
    // no test-result should be emitted
    expect(emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'test-result').length).to.equal(0);
  });

  it('jasmine2 suppresses and signals all-test-results once', function() {
    let env = { addReporter: function() {} };
    let added;
    env.addReporter = function(r) { added = r; };
    replaceGlobals({ jasmine: { getEnv: function() { return env; } } }, globals);
    jasmine2Adapter();
    added.jasmineStarted();
    added.specDone({ status: 'failed', fullName: 'x', id: 0, failedExpectations: [] });
    added.jasmineDone();
    let all = emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'all-test-results');
    expect(all.length).to.equal(1);
    expect(emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'test-result').length).to.equal(0);
  });

  it('qunit suppresses, clears queue, signals all-test-results once', function() {
    let queue = [1,2,3];
    let cfg = { queue: queue };
    let Q = {
      config: cfg,
      log: function(){}, testStart: function(){}, testDone: function(){}, done: function(){}
    };
    let testDone, done;
    Q.testStart = function(fn){};
    Q.testDone = function(fn){ testDone = fn; };
    Q.done = function(fn){ done = fn; };
    replaceGlobals({ QUnit: Q }, globals);
    qunitAdapter();
    testDone({ name: 'x', failed: 1, passed: 0, skipped: 0, todo: 0, total: 1, runtime: 1, module: '', testId: 1 });
    done({ runtime: 1 });
    expect(queue.length).to.equal(0);
    let all = emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'all-test-results');
    expect(all.length).to.equal(1);
    expect(emit.getCalls().filter(c => c.args[0] === 'test-result').length).to.equal(0);
  });
});
EOF

'use strict';
const expect = require('/app/node_modules/chai').expect;
const PassThrough = require('stream').PassThrough;
const Config = require('/app/lib/config');
const TapReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/tap_reporter');
const DotReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/dot_reporter');
const TeamcityReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/teamcity_reporter');
const XUnitReporter = require('/app/lib/reporters/xunit_reporter');

function bailedApp() {
  return {
    reporter: {
      hasBailed: function() { return true; },
      getBailReport: function() {
        return {
          testsRanBeforeBail: 2,
          bailLauncher: 'launcher',
          failuresByLauncher: { launcher: 1 },
          failedTests: ['f1']
        };
      },
      bailReason: 'f1',
      suppressed: 3
    }
  };
}

describe('direct reporter bail output', function() {
  it('tap summary on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let tap = new TapReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    tap.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
    expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 2');
    expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 3');
  });
  it('dot summary on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let dot = new DotReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    dot.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('# bailed');
    expect(out).to.contain('# ran before bail 2');
    expect(out).to.contain('# suppressed 3');
  });
  it('teamcity on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let tc = new TeamcityReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    tc.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('Bail out!');
    expect(out).to.contain("key='bailedTests' value='1'");
    expect(out).to.contain("key='testsBeforeBail' value='2'");
    expect(out).to.contain("key='suppressedAfterBail' value='3'");
    expect(out).to.contain('buildProblem');
  });
  it('xunit on finish', function() {
    let s = new PassThrough();
    let xu = new XUnitReporter(false, s, new Config('ci'), bailedApp());
    xu.finish();
    let out = s.read().toString();
    expect(out).to.contain('errors="1"');
    expect(out).to.contain('<error');
    expect(out).to.contain('name="bailReason" value="f1"');
    expect(out).to.contain('name="testsBeforeBail" value="2"');
    expect(out).to.contain('name="suppressedAfterBail" value="3"');
    expect(out).to.contain('<system-out>');
  });
});
EOF

# Update mockApp to support bail_on_test_failure
s=s.replace("""  function mockApp(reporter) {
    reporter = reporter || new FakeReporter();

    return {
      config: {
        get: function(key) {
          switch (key) {
            case 'reporter':
              return reporter;
          }
        }
      }
    };
  }
""", """  function mockApp(reporter, bailOnTestFailure) {
    reporter = reporter || new FakeReporter();

    return {
      config: {
        get: function(key) {
          switch (key) {
            case 'reporter':
              return reporter;
            case 'bail_on_test_failure':
              return bailOnTestFailure;
          }
        }
      }
    };
  }
""", 1)

s += r"""
  describe('bail_on_test_failure', function() {
    let reporter;

    it('bails on the first failure when configured with true', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, true), stream);

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'test1', passed: false });

      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.true();
      expect(reporter.bailReason).to.equal('test1');
      expect(reporter.bailLauncher).to.equal('launcher');
      expect(reporter.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(0);
      expect(reporter.getBailReport()).to.deep.equal({
        testsRanBeforeBail: 0,
        bailLauncher: 'launcher',
        failuresByLauncher: { launcher: 1 },
        failedTests: ['test1']
      });
    });

    it('bails on the Nth failure for a launcher when configured with N', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, 2), stream);

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'test1', passed: false });
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.false();

      reporter.report('other', { name: 'test2', passed: false });
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.false();

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'test3', passed: false });
      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.true();
      expect(reporter.bailReason).to.equal('test3');
      expect(reporter.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(2);
    });

    it('does not count skipped or todo results as failures', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, true), stream);

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'skipped', skipped: true });
      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'todo', todo: true, passed: false });

      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.false();
    });

    it('suppresses results from sub-reporters after bailing', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, true), stream);
      let subReporter = sandbox.spy(reporter.reporters[0], 'report');

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'fail', passed: false });
      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'after', passed: true });

      expect(subReporter).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(reporter.suppressed).to.equal(1);
    });

    it('does not bail when configured with false', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, false), stream);

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'test1', passed: false });

      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.false();
    });

    it('warns and defaults to false for invalid values', function() {
      let warn = sandbox.stub(require('npmlog'), 'warn');

      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, 0), stream);
      expect(reporter.bailOnFailures).to.equal(false);
      expect(warn).to.have.been.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure');

      warn.resetHistory();
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, 'yes'), stream);
      expect(reporter.bailOnFailures).to.equal(false);
      expect(warn).to.have.been.calledWith('bail_on_test_failure');
    });

    it('clears all bail state when reset', function() {
      reporter = new Reporter(mockApp(null, true), stream);

      reporter.report('launcher', { name: 'test1', passed: false });
      reporter.resetBailState();

      expect(reporter.hasBailed()).to.be.false();
      expect(reporter.bailReason).to.equal(null);
      expect(reporter.bailLauncher).to.equal(null);
      expect(reporter.testsRanBeforeBail).to.equal(0);
      expect(reporter.failuresByLauncher).to.deep.equal({});
      expect(reporter.failedTests).to.deep.equal([]);
      expect(reporter.suppressed).to.equal(0);
    });
  });
});
"""
open(p,'w').write(s)

      var output = stream.read().toString();

      var output = stream.read().toString();

  describe('bail on test failure', function() {

      config = new Config('ci');
      app = new App(config);
      app.reporter = { resetBailState: sandbox.stub() };
      app.server = {
        broadcastAbort: sandbox.stub(),

      app.reporter = reporter;
      let server = { resetAbort: sandbox.stub(), broadcastAbort: sandbox.stub() };
      app.server = server;
      app.aborted = true;

      app.resetBailState();

      expect(app.aborted).to.be.false();
      expect(server.resetAbort).to.have.been.calledOnce();
      expect(reporter.resetBailState).to.have.been.calledOnce();
    });
  });
});

cd /app && nl -ba README.md | sed -n '242,330p'
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
   242	Configuration File
   243	------------------
   244	
   245	For the simplest JavaScript projects, the TDD workflow described above will work fine. There are times when you want
   246	to structure your source files into separate directories, or want to have finer control over what files to include.
   247	This calls for the `testem.json` configuration file (you can also alternatively use the YAML format with a `testem.yml` file). It looks like
   248	
   249	```json
   250	{
   251	  "framework": "jasmine",
   252	  "src_files": [
   253	    "hello.js",
   254	    "hello_spec.js"
   255	  ]
   256	}
   257	```
   258	
   259	The `src_files` can also be unix glob patterns.
   260	
   261	```json
   262	{
   263	  "src_files": [
   264	    "js/**/*.js",
   265	    "spec/**/*.js"
   266	  ]
   267	}
   268	```
   269	
   270	You can also ignore certain files using `src_files_ignore`.
   271	***Update: I've removed the ability to use a space-separated list of globs as a string in the `src_files` property because it disallowed matching files or directories with spaces in them.***
   272	
   273	```json
   274	{
   275	  "src_files": [
   276	    "js/**/*.js",
   277	    "spec/**/*.js"
   278	  ],
   279	  "src_files_ignore": "js/toxic/*.js"
   280	}
   281	```
   282	
   283	Read [more details](docs/config_file.md) about the config options.
   284	
   285	Custom Test Pages
   286	-----------------
   287	
   288	You can also use a custom page for testing. To do this, first you need to specify `test_page` to point to your test page in the config file (`framework` and `src_files` are irrelevant in this case)
   289	
   290	```json
   291	{
   292	  "test_page": "tests.html",
   293	  "launch_in_dev": [
   294	    "Chrome"
   295	  ]
   296	}
   297	```
   298	
   299	Next, the test page you use needs to have the adapter code installed on them, as specified in the next section.
   300	
   301	### Include Snippet
   302	
   303	Include this snippet directly after your `jasmine.js`, `qunit.js` or `mocha.js` scripts to enable *Testem* with your test page.
   304	
   305	```html
   306	<script src="/testem.js"></script>
   307	```
   308	
   309	Or if you are using require.js or another loader, just make sure you load `/testem.js` as the next script after the test framework.
   310	
   311	'/testem.js' here is dynamically generated to be used client-side and it should not be confused with server-side 'testem.js'.
   312	
   313	### Dynamic Substitution
   314	
   315	To enable dynamic substitutions within the Javascript files in your custom test page, you must
   316	
   317	1. name your test page using `.mustache` as the extension
   318	2. use `{{#serve_files}}` to loop over the set of Javascript files to be served, and then reference its `src` property to access their path (or `{{#css_files}}` for stylesheets)
   319	
   320	Example:
   321	
   322	    {{#serve_files}}
   323	    <script src="{{src}}"></script>
   324	    {{/serve_files}}
   325	
   326	    {{#css_files}}
   327	    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{{src}}">
   328	    {{/css_files}}
   329	
   330	### Multiple Test Pages

END STEP

/*
  CLI-level options:

  file:                 [String]  configuration file (testem.json, .testem.json, testem.yml, .testem.yml)
  host:                 [String]  server host to use (localhost)
  port:                 [Number]  server port to use (7357)
  launch:               [Array]   list of launchers to use for current runs (defaults to current mode)
  skip:                 [Array]   list of launchers to skip
  debug:                [Boolean] debug mode (false)
  test_page:            [String|Array]  path (or list of paths) to the page (or pages) to use to run tests
  growl:                [Boolean] enables growl / native notifications (false)

  Config-level options:

  launchers:            [Object]  List of custom launchers
  launch_in_dev:        [Array]   list of launchers to use for dev runs
  launch_in_ci:         [Array]   list of launchers to use for CI runs

  src_files_ignore:     [Array]   list of files or file patterns to exclude from usage
  serve_files:          [Array]   list of files or file patterns to inject into test playground (defaults to src_files)
  watch_files:          [Array]   list of files or file patterns to watch changes of (defaults to src_files)
  css_files:            [Array]   additional stylesheets to include
  cwd:                  [Path]    directory to use as root
  config_dir:           [Path]    directory to use as root for resolving configs, if different than cwd
  query_params:         [Object]  Object map defining query params to add to the test page. Can be query string also.
  parallel:             [Number]  max number of parallel runners (1)
  routes:               [Object]  overrides for assets paths
  fail_on_zero_tests:   [Boolean] whether process should exit with error status when no tests found
  unsafe_file_serving:  [Boolean] allow serving directories that are not in your CWD (false)

  Available hooks:

  on_start:             Runs on suite startup
  before_tests:         Runs before every run of tests
  after_tests:          Runs after every run of tests
  on_exit:              Runs before suite exits
*/

class Api {
  setup(mode, finalizer) {
    this.config = new Config(mode, this.options);
    this.config.setDefaultOptions(this.defaultOptions);
    this.configureLogging();
    log.info('Test\'em starting..');
    this.config.read(() => {
      this.app = new App(this.config, finalizer);
      this.app.start();
    });
  }

  configureLogging() {
    let debug = this.config.get('debug');
    if (debug) {
      log.stream = fs.createWriteStream(debug);
    } else {
      let fakeStream = new EventEmitter();
      fakeStream.write = () => {};
      log.stream = fakeStream;
    }
  }

  startDev(options, finalizer) {
    this.options = options;
    this.setup('dev', finalizer);
  }

  restart() {
    this.app.triggerRun('Api: restart');

END STEP

+  bailOut() {
+    this.emitBail();
+  }
+
+  emitBail() {
+    if (this.silent || this.bailReported) {
+      return;
+    }
+    this.bailReported = true;
+    let report = this.getBailReport();
+    let count = report.failuresByLauncher[report.bailLauncher] || 0;
+    let reason = escape(this.app.reporter.bailReason);
+    this.out.write(teamcityLine('message', {
+      text: 'Bail out!',
+      errorDetails: reason,
+      status: 'ERROR'
+    }));
+    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
+      key: 'bailedTests',
+      value: count
+    }));
+    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
+      key: 'testsBeforeBail',
+      value: report.testsRanBeforeBail
+    }));
+    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildStatisticValue', {
+      key: 'suppressedAfterBail',
+      value: this.getSuppressed()
+    }));
+    this.out.write(teamcityLine('buildProblem', {
+      description: `Bail out! ${reason}`
+    }));
+  }
+
+  hasBailed() {
+    return !!(this.app && this.app.reporter && this.app.reporter.hasBailed());
+  }
+
+  getBailReport() {
+    return this.app.reporter.getBailReport();
+  }
+
+  getSuppressed() {
+    return this.app.reporter.suppressed;
+  }
+
   finish() {
     if (this.silent) {
       return;
     }
     this.endTime = new Date();
+    if (this.hasBailed()) {
+      this.emitBail();
+    }
     this.out.write('\n\n');
     this.out.write(teamcityLine('testSuiteFinished', {
       name: 'testem.suite',

STEP 154 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && git add -A && git commit -m "Add bail_on_test_failure support

- Add bail_on_test_failure config default (false); true means threshold 1,
  positive integer N means threshold N. Reporter validates the value and
  warns via npmlog with bail_on_test_failure prefix for invalid values.
- Reporter becomes an EventEmitter that bails on the Nth non-skipped
  non-todo failure per launcher, records bailReason, emits test-failure,
  gates subsequent sub-reporter results, and exposes hasBailed,
  getBailReport, and resetBailState.
- Add abort support to runners: idempotent Promise-returning abort that
  suppresses subsequent results/errors; browser runners emit abort-tests.
- Server gains idempotent broadcastAbort (tolerating uninitialized io) and
  resetAbort. App gains idempotent abortRunners, resetBailState, and a
  bail-specific getExitCode error.
- TAP and Dot output Bail out! plus summary lines; Teamcity emits bail
  message, statistics, and build problem; XUnit adds error element, errors
  attribute, properties, and system-out summary.
- Client handles abort-tests by setting aborted, emitting abort-tests and
  after-tests-complete, and blocking further emitMessage; connection iframe
  relays abort-tests. Mocha/Jasmine2/QUnit adapters guard all emission
  points and signal all-test-results once (QUnit also clears its queue)." && git log --oneline -3
